### Part 1: 找到 4 个原始 JSONL 文件

在 **两台服务器** 上搜索 DocRED LoRA greedy 输出文件。

#### westd-16980:
```bash
# 搜索所有可能的 w5 和 DocRED greedy 输出
find /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/ -name "*.jsonl" | xargs ls -la | grep -i "docred.*greedy\|w5.*docred\|llama.*docred.*greedy\|qwen.*docred.*greedy"
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/ | grep -i "w5\|docred.*lora\|docred.*greedy\|docred.*ft"
find /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/ -name "*docred*greedy*.jsonl" -o -name "*w5*docred*.jsonl" -o -name "*docred*lora*.jsonl" 2>/dev/null
```
也搜索 outputs/ 和 OntoCoDec 目录：
```bash
find /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/outputs/ -name "*docred*greedy*.jsonl" -o -name "*w5*docred*.jsonl" 2>/dev/null
find /root/autodl-tmp/OntoCoDec/outputs/ -name "*docred*.jsonl" 2>/dev/null
```

#### westd-21461:
```bash
find /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/ -name "*docred*greedy*.jsonl" -o -name "*w5*docred*.jsonl" -o -name "*docred*lora*.jsonl" 2>/dev/null
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/ | grep -i "docred.*greedy\|w5\|d065"
find /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/outputs/ -name "*docred*greedy*.jsonl" 2>/dev/null
```

### Part 2: 确认文件是哪个（每个文件检查行数和模型）

对找到的每个候选文件：
```bash
wc -l &lt;file>
head -1 &lt;file> | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.loads(sys.stdin.readline()); print(f'model={d.get(\"model\",\"?\")}, mode={d.get(\"mode\",\"?\")}, doc_id={d.get(\"doc_id\",\"?\")}')"
```

我们需要的 4 个文件是 998-doc（全量 DocRED dev）的 greedy 输出：
- Qwen LoRA DocRED unconstrained (998 行)
- Qwen LoRA DocRED constrained/full_s1 (998 行) — 注意：这个正在 rerun 中（d065_r2），可能还没完成。如果没有完整的 998 行 con 文件，先 eval 另外 3 个，标注 Qwen con 待 rerun 完成。
- Llama LoRA DocRED unconstrained (998 行)
- Llama LoRA DocRED constrained/full_s1 (998 行)

Table 2 可能用的是较早的文件（如 w5 系列，可能不是 998 行而是某个 subset）。**不要假设行数，看实际文件。** 如果 Table 2 用的是非 998 行的文件，也要找到它。

### Part 3: 用 eval_d064.py 对 4 个文件 re-eval

先确认 eval_d064.py 的位置和用法：
```bash
find /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/ -name "eval_d064.py" 2>/dev/null
python3 /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/eval_d064.py --help 2>/dev/null | head -20
```

对每个文件运行 eval（在文件所在的服务器上执行）：
```bash
cd /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie && python3 eval_d064.py \
  --input &lt;JSONL_FILE_PATH> \
  --dataset docred 2>&1
```

完整捕获输出，包含所有指标：E-F1, E-P, E-R, R-F1, R-P, R-R, DR%, RV%, CF%, ET%, PF%（parse failure rate），pred_entities, pred_relations。

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"{\\\"doc_id\\\": \\\"Skai TV\\\", \\\"input_text\\\": \\\"Skai TV is a Greek free - to - air television network based in Piraeus . It is part of the Skai Group , one of the largest media groups in the country . It was relaunched in its present form on 1st of April 2006 in the Athens metropolitan area , and gradually spread its coverage nationwide . Besides digital terrestrial transmission , it is available on the subscription - based encrypted services of Nova and Cosmote TV . Skai TV is also a member of Digea , a consortium of private television networks introducing digital terrestrial transmission in Greece . At launch , Skai TV opted for dubbing all foreign language content into Greek , instead of using subtitles . This is very uncommon in Greece for anything except documentaries ( using voiceover dubbing ) and children 's programmes ( using lip - synced dubbing ) , so after intense criticism the station **Llama DocRED:**
| Metric | tex | eval | Match? |
|--------|-----|------|--------|

| CF | --- | 0.08 | (tex says not computed) |
| R-F1 | 43.72 | 43.72 | YES |
| E-F1 | 83.93 | 83.93 | YES |

I'm tracking down where the 44.23 value came from — it appears in the text as the Llama constrained DocRED R-F1, but that same metric from the evaluation file shows 43.78. The 44.23 actually refers to beam search results on SciERC, not DocRED, so it seems the tex may have mixed up values from different datasets or configurations.
